04:07:42.479 using functions to transform data. Object-oriented programming is using classes to bundle data and behavior. So
04:07:49.840 here's the recap for now. So both approaches achieve the same result. Classes don't unlock new features in

04:08:02.479 level that you're confident with when you're writing and working with your Python files. As a general rule of thumb, you want to use classes when you
04:08:09.600 want to keep track of a state between operations. So you want to save data, h group related data and functions together for organization, create
04:08:16.399 multiple instances with similar behavior or model real world objects or concepts. And you can use functions when it's
04:08:22.239 about simple transformations, input, output, stateless operations, so it doesn't need to remember anything. one-off calculations or small scripts.
